></div>\n\n</section>\n\n<div class=\"relative\">\n\n    \n            <div class=\"contained relative z-20\">\n          <div class=\"flex flex-col xl:flex-row w-full xl:mb-16\">\n\n            <div class=\"w-full shrink xl:w-3/4 xl:pr-20 xl:mb-12\">\n              <article class=\"w-full\">\n  \n  \n  \n    \n    <div\n      class=\"relative z-20 scroll-target\"\n\n      >\n      <div \n         data-lazy class=\"\"\n\n      \n      >\n        <div class=\"\">\n        <div class=\"w-full relative z-20 max-w-screen-2xl mx-auto\" >\n          \n          \n            \n            <div class=\"w-full relative mt-4 mb-8 lg:mb-16\"\n\n              \n              >\n\n              \n              \n                              \n                  \n                                        <div\n    tabindex=\"0\"\n    class=\"prose md:prose-lg dark:prose-invert text-pretty w-full max-w-none\"\n  >\n    <p>Circuit Civil Department maintains the records and documentation for cases involving lawsuits greater than $50,000. Other types of cases handled by Circuit Civil are mortgage and lien foreclosure, medical and professional malpractice, auto negligence, contract and indebtedness, personal injury, and eminent domain.</p>\n<p>The public may view most Circuit Civil case files unless the case has been sealed. In which case, it cannot be viewed without a court order.</p>\n<p>If you require legal advice or need information not provided here, you may need to consult an attorney licensed in the State of Florida or some other qualified source such as the <a href=\"https://www.floridabar.org/directories/find-mbr/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" aria-label=\"Florida Bar (opens in a new tab)\">Florida Bar</a>.</p>\n  </div>\n\n  \n                  </div>\n          \n            \n            <div class=\"w-full relative mt-4\"\n\n              \n              >\n\n              \n              \n                              \n                  \n                                      \n    \n  <h2    class=\"heading page-h2 text-left\"\n    tabindex=\"0\"\n  >\n  Frequently Asked Questions  </h2>\n                    \n                  \n                                      \n\n\n        <div class=\"my-4 w-full max-w-none space-y-4 mb-8\">\n\n    \n      \n      <div id=\"panel_1221\" class=\"bg-[#EEF1F6] border-l-4 border-[#7183A3]\"  itemscope itemprop=\"mainEntity\" itemtype=\"https://schema.org/Question\" >\n\n        \n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          class=\"group relative w-full flex items-center justify-between gap-6 px-6 py-5 text-left transition-all duration-300 after:content-[''] after:absolute after:left-6 after:right-6 after:bottom-0 after:h-px after:bg-gradient-to-r after:from-[#B5921C] after:to-transparent after:transition-opacity after:duration-300 after:pointer-events-none\"\n          :class=\"{ 'after:opacity-0': !openPanels.includes('panel_1221'), 'after:opacity-100': openPanels.includes('panel_1221') }\"\n          :aria-expanded=\"openPanels.includes('panel_1221') ?    <p>The Florida Bar is able to provide legal referrals. You can contact the Florida Bar by phone at <a href=\"tel:8505615600\" rel=\"nofollow\">(850) 561-5600</a> or online at <a href=\"https://www.floridabar.org/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" aria-label=\"floridabar.org (opens in a new tab)\">floridabar.org</a>. Click on the “Find a Lawyer” button, then click on the “Lawyer Referral Service Site” link.</p>\n          </div>\n\n        </div>\n\n      </div>\n\n    \n      \n      <div id=\"panel_1223\" class=\"bg-[#EEF1F6] border-l-4 border-[#7183A3]\"  itemscope itemprop=\"mainEntity\" itemtype=\"https://schema.org/Question\" >\n\n        \n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          class=\"group relative w-full flex items-center justify-between gap-6 px-6 py-5 text-left transition-all duration-300 after:content-[''] after:absolute after:left-6 after:right-6 after:bottom-0 after:h-px after:bg-gradient-to-r after:from-[#B5921C] after:to-transparent after:transition-opacity after:duration-300 after:pointer-events-none\"\n          :class=\"{ 'after:opacity-0': !openPanels.includes('panel_1223'), 'after:opacity-100': openPanels.includes('panel_1223') }\"\n          :aria-expanded=\"openPanels.includes('panel_1223') ?          \n          <span\n            class=\"flex-none flex items-center justify-center h-6 w-6 rounded-full border border-[#7183A3] text-accent transition-transform duration-300\"\n stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-width=\"2.5\"/>\n  </g>\n</svg>\n            </span>\n          </span>\n\n        </button>\n\n        \n        <div\n          id=\"panel_content_1_3\"\n          class=\"w-full overflow-hidden bg-[#EEF1F6] transition-all duration-500\"\n          v-bind:class=\"{ 'h-0 opacity-0 invisible': !openPanels.includes('panel_1223'), 'h-auto opacity-100 visible': openPanels.includes('panel_1223') }\"\n           itemscope itemprop=\"acceptedAnswer\" itemtype=\"https://schema.org/Answer\" >\n\n          <div\n            class=\"prose max-w-none px-6 pt-4 pb-6 prose-p:text-[18px] prose-p:leading-[29px] [&_ul]:my-2 [&_ol]:my-2 [&_ul]:pl-5 [&_ol]:pl-5 [&_li]:!text-[16px] [&_li]:!leading-[25px] [&_li]:!font-medium [&_li]:!tracking-[0.2px] [&_li]:!text-[#1D376C] [&_li::marker]:!text-[#B5921C] [&_li::marker]:!text-[14px]\"\n            v-bind:ref=\"'panel_content_1_3'\"\n             itemprop=\"text\" >\n            <p>You can have your summons (issued to you by the Clerk’s Office) served by the Sheriff’s Office or a private process server. You cannot have a court hearing until the person or business you are suing is served.</p>\n<p>You must know the correct name of the person, business, or corporation you are suing as well as their address and phone number, if possible. If the defendant is not served, you may attempt service again when you have located a new address.</p>\n<p>If you are suing a business, you should contact the Secretary of State Website at <a href=\"http://www.sunbiz.org/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" aria-label=\"sunbiz.org (opens in a new tab)\">sunbiz.org</a> to retrieve the information needed to have your summons served on the Registered Agent of the corporation or business.</p>\n<ul>\n<li><strong>Sheriff’s Service Fee:</strong> The Sheriff’s service fee is $40.00 per defendant for Florida residents. Out-of-state Sheriff’s departments may charge a different fee. You are required to have a money order or business check made payable to the Sheriff of the County where the resident resides. For more information, please visit the Putnam County Sheriff’s website at <a href=\"https://www.putnamsherifffl.com/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" aria-label=\"gladessheriff.org (opens in a new tab)\">putnamsherifffl.com</a>.</li>\n<li><strong>Private Process Server:</strong> You will need to contact the process server of your choice to determine the cost of service. The Clerk’s Office cannot refer a process server to you. You can get a referral from the Florida Association of Professional Process Servers at <a href=\"https://fapps.org/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" aria-label=\"fapps.org (opens in a new tab)\">fapps.org</a>, which provides a “Find A Process Server” link on their website.</li>\n</ul>\n<p>If you have any questions, please call our office at <a href=\"tel:3863267600\" rel=\"nofollow\">(386) 326-7600</a>.</p>\n          </div>\n\n        </div>\n\n      </div>\n\n    \n      \n      <div id=\"panel_1224\" class=\"bg-[#EEF1F6] border-l-4 border-[#7183A3]\"  itemscope itemprop=\"mainEntity\" itemtype=\"https://schema.org/Question\" >\n\n        \n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          class=\"group relative w-full flex items-center justify-between gap-6 px-6 py-5 text-left transition-all duration-300 after:content-[''] after:absolute after:left-6 after:right-6 after:bottom-0 after:h-px after:bg-gradient-to-r after:from-[#B5921C] after:to-transparent after:transition-opacity after:duration-300 after:pointer-events-none\"\n          :class=\"{ 'after:opacity-0': !openPanels.includes('panel_1224'), 'after:opacity-100': openPanels.includes('panel_1224') }\"\n          :aria-expanded=\"openPanels.includes('panel_1224') ? Other types of cases handled by Circuit Civil are mortgage and lien foreclosure, medical and professional malpractice, auto negligence, contract and indebtedness, personal injury, and eminent domain.\n\nThe public may view most Circuit Civil case files unless the case has been sealed. In which case, it cannot be viewed without a court order.\n\nIf you require legal advice or need information not provided here, you may need to consult an attorney licensed in the State of Florida or some other qualified source such as the Florida Bar.\n\nFrequently Asked Questions\nWhat types of cases are most commonly filed in the Circuit Civil division?\nHow do I find an attorney?\nHow do I find someone to serve my documents?\nHelpful Resources\nOTHER SERVICES\nCOURTHOUSE\nCOMPLEX\n\n107 6th St, Palatka, FL\n(Customer Service Center)\n\n410 St Johns Ave, Palatka, FL\n(Courthouse)\n\nMonday- Friday\n8:30 AM – 5:00 PM\n\nCALL OUR OFFICE\nGOVERNMENT\nCOMPLEX\n\n2509 Crill Ave, Suite 300\nPalatka, FL 32177\n\nMonday, Wednesday, & Friday\n8:30 AM – 5:00 PM\n\nCALL OUR OFFICE\nCRESCENT CITY\nSATELLITE OFFICE\n\n115 N Summit St\nCrescent City, FL 32112\n\nTuesday, Wednesday, & Thursday\n9:00 AM – 4:30 PM\n\nCALL OUR OFFICE\nINTERLACHEN\nSATELLITE OFFICE\n\n1114 State Road 20\nInterlachen, FL 32148\n\nTuesday & Thursday\n9:00 AM – 4:30 PM\n\nCALL OUR OFFICE\nMAILING ADDRESS\n\nP.O.
